      var error = payload.error || {};
      var error_msg = error.message || error.stack || '';
      // --- 修改点：兼容两种错误模式的正则 ---

      // ----------------------------------
      var stackMatchList = url && url.match(/.+\/([^/.]+)\.([^/.]+)\.(js|css)$/);

        );
        extra.template_key = template_key;
        extra.card_version = card_version;

        if (ev_type === 'js_error') {
          var retry = retryMap[template_key] || 0;
